\begin{lexlean}{SemanticDecode}
\useglossary{lexlean.std.nat@1.2.0}
\importmodule{SemanticDecls}
\importmodule{SemanticModels}
\importmodule{SemanticReasoning}
\importmodule{SemanticTerms}
\importmodule{SemanticTypes}
\importmodule{LexLeanStd.Json}
\importmodule{LexLeanStd.Numeral}
\importmodule{LexLeanStd.Order}
\importmodule{LexLeanStd.Text}
\title{Natural number addition}
\begin{semanticmodule}
inductive{
  name: LooseFrame,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: LooseArr, fields: [LexLeanStd.Json::Json]},
    {name: LooseObj, fields: [Bytes, LexLeanStd.Json::Json]}
  ]
}

inductive{
  name: LooseReading,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [{name: LooseRead, fields: [Nat, Nat, (List LooseFrame), LexLeanStd.Json::Json]}]
}

definition{
  name: byteAt,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, position : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match (prim index text position : (Option UInt8))
    | Option.some byte => (prim to_nat byte : Nat)
    | Option.none => 256),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: isDigit,
  parameters: [value : Nat],
  result: Bool,
  body: ((47 <? value) && (value <? 58)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: digitAt,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, position : Nat],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (if (isDigit (byteAt text position)) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (position + 1)) else (ctor Option.none [Nat])),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: digitsUntil,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, start : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (first (prim iterate_until (fun [text] (position : Nat) => (digitAt text position)) ((prim length text : Nat) + 1) start : (Nat * Bool))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseInteger,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, position : Nat],
  result: (Option (Nat * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)),
  body: (let negative : Bool := ((byteAt text position) =? 45); (let start : Nat := (if negative then (position + 1) else position); (let stop : Nat := (digitsUntil text start); (let next : Nat := (byteAt text stop); (if ((stop =? start) || ((((byteAt text start) =? 48) && (1 <? (prim subtract stop start : Nat))) || ((next =? 46) || ((next =? 101) || (next =? 69))))) then (ctor Option.none [(Nat * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)]) else (match (LexLeanStd.Numeral::parseDecimal (LexLeanStd.Text::sliceOr text start (prim subtract stop start : Nat)))
    | Option.some magnitude => (ctor Option.some [(Nat * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)] (stop, (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt (if (magnitude =? 0) then false else negative) magnitude)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [(Nat * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)]))))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: hexDigit,
  parameters: [value : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (if (isDigit value) then (prim subtract value 48 : Nat) else (if ((64 <? value) && (value <? 71)) then (prim subtract value 55 : Nat) else (if ((96 <? value) && (value <? 103)) then (prim subtract value 87 : Nat) else 16))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: hexAt,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, position : Nat],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (let d0 : Nat := (hexDigit (byteAt text position)); (let d1 : Nat := (hexDigit (byteAt text (position + 1))); (let d2 : Nat := (hexDigit (byteAt text (position + 2))); (let d3 : Nat := (hexDigit (byteAt text (position + 3))); (if ((15 <? d0) || ((15 <? d1) || ((15 <? d2) || (15 <? d3)))) then (ctor Option.none [Nat]) else (ctor Option.some [Nat] ((((prim multiply d0 4096 : Nat) + (prim multiply d1 256 : Nat)) + (prim multiply d2 16 : Nat)) + d3))))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: byteTable,
  parameters: [],
  result: Bytes,
  body: 0x000102030405060708090a0b0c0d0e0f101112131415161718191a1b1c1d1e1f202122232425262728292a2b2c2d2e2f303132333435363738393a3b3c3d3e3f404142434445464748494a4b4c4d4e4f505152535455565758595a5b5c5d5e5f606162636465666768696a6b6c6d6e6f707172737475767778797a7b7c7d7e7f808182838485868788898a8b8c8d8e8f909192939495969798999a9b9c9d9e9fa0a1a2a3a4a5a6a7a8a9aaabacadaeafb0b1b2b3b4b5b6b7b8b9babbbcbdbebfc0c1c2c3c4c5c6c7c8c9cacbcccdcecfd0d1d2d3d4d5d6d7d8d9dadbdcdddedfe0e1e2e3e4e5e6e7e8e9eaebecedeeeff0f1f2f3f4f5f6f7f8f9fafbfcfdfeff,
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: oneByte,
  parameters: [value : Nat],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (LexLeanStd.Text::sliceOr (byteTable) value 1),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: trailing,
  parameters: [code : Nat, scale : Nat],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (oneByte (128 + (prim remainder (prim quotient code scale 0 : Nat) 64 0 : Nat))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: utf8Of,
  parameters: [code : Nat],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (if (code <? 128) then (oneByte code) else (if (code <? 2048) then (prim append (oneByte (192 + (prim quotient code 64 0 : Nat))) (trailing code 1) : Bytes) else (if (code <? 65536) then (prim append (prim append (oneByte (224 + (prim quotient code 4096 0 : Nat))) (trailing code 64) : Bytes) (trailing code 1) : Bytes) else (prim append (prim append (prim append (oneByte (240 + (prim quotient code 262144 0 : Nat))) (trailing code 4096) : Bytes) (trailing code 64) : Bytes) (trailing code 1) : Bytes)))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: plainEscape,
  parameters: [letter : Nat],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (if (letter =? 34) then 0x22 else (if (letter =? 92) then 0x5c else (if (letter =? 47) then 0x2f else (if (letter =? 98) then 0x08 else (if (letter =? 102) then 0x0c else (if (letter =? 110) then 0x0a else (if (letter =? 114) then 0x0d else (if (letter =? 116) then 0x09 else 0x)))))))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: unicodeEscape,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, position : Nat],
  result: (Option (Nat * Bytes)),
  body: (match (hexAt text (position + 2))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [(Nat * Bytes)])
    | Option.some high => (if ((55295 <? high) && (high <? 56320)) then (if (((byteAt text (position + 6)) =? 92) && ((byteAt text (position + 7)) =? 117)) then (match (hexAt text (position + 8))
      | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [(Nat * Bytes)])
      | Option.some low => (if ((56319 <? low) && (low <? 57344)) then (ctor Option.some [(Nat * Bytes)] ((position + 12), (utf8Of ((65536 + (prim multiply (prim subtract high 55296 : Nat) 1024 : Nat)) + (prim subtract low 56320 : Nat))))) else (ctor Option.none [(Nat * Bytes)]))) else (ctor Option.none [(Nat * Bytes)])) else (if ((56319 <? high) && (high <? 57344)) then (ctor Option.none [(Nat * Bytes)]) else (ctor Option.some [(Nat * Bytes)] ((position + 6), (utf8Of high)))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseEscape,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, position : Nat, out : Bytes],
  result: (Option (Nat * (Bytes * Nat))),
  body: (let letter : Nat := (byteAt text (position + 1)); (if (letter =? 117) then (match (unicodeEscape text position)
    | Option.some decoded => (ctor Option.some [(Nat * (Bytes * Nat))] ((first decoded), ((prim append out (second decoded) : Bytes), 0)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.some [(Nat * (Bytes * Nat))] (position, (out, 2)))) else (let piece : Bytes := (plainEscape letter); (if (prim equal piece 0x : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [(Nat * (Bytes * Nat))] (position, (out, 2))) else (ctor Option.some [(Nat * (Bytes * Nat))] ((position + 2), ((prim append out piece : Bytes), 0))))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseStringStep,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, state : (Nat * (Bytes * Nat))],
  result: (Option (Nat * (Bytes * Nat))),
  body: (let position : Nat := (first state); (let out : Bytes := (first (second state)); (if ((second (second state)) =? 0) then (let byte : Nat := (byteAt text position); (if (byte =? 34) then (ctor Option.some [(Nat * (Bytes * Nat))] ((position + 1), (out, 1))) else (if (byte =? 92) then (looseEscape text position out) else (if ((byte <? 32) || (byte =? 256)) then (ctor Option.some [(Nat * (Bytes * Nat))] (position, (out, 2))) else (ctor Option.some [(Nat * (Bytes * Nat))] ((position + 1), ((prim append out (LexLeanStd.Text::sliceOr text position 1) : Bytes), 0))))))) else (ctor Option.none [(Nat * (Bytes * Nat))])))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseString,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, start : Nat],
  result: (Option (Nat * Bytes)),
  body: (let done : ((Nat * (Bytes * Nat)) * Bool) := (prim iterate_until (fun [text] (state : (Nat * (Bytes * Nat))) => (looseStringStep text state)) ((prim length text : Nat) + 1) (start, (0x, 0)) : ((Nat * (Bytes * Nat)) * Bool)); (if ((second (second (first done))) =? 1) then (ctor Option.some [(Nat * Bytes)] ((first (first done)), (first (second (first done))))) else (ctor Option.none [(Nat * Bytes)]))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: spineStep,
  parameters: [state : (LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)],
  result: (Option (LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)),
  body: (match (first state)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => (ctor Option.none [(LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => (ctor Option.none [(LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => (ctor Option.none [(LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr inner => (ctor Option.none [(LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj inner => (ctor Option.none [(LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => (ctor Option.none [(LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => (ctor Option.some [(LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)] (rest, (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element (second state))))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => (ctor Option.some [(LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)] (rest, (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner (second state))))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: reversedSpine,
  parameters: [spine : LexLeanStd.Json::Json, fuel : Nat],
  result: LexLeanStd.Json::Json,
  body: (second (first (prim iterate_until (fun (state : (LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json)) => (spineStep state)) fuel (spine, (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil)) : ((LexLeanStd.Json::Json * LexLeanStd.Json::Json) * Bool)))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseBroken,
  parameters: [position : Nat],
  result: LooseReading,
  body: (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead position 9 (nil LooseFrame) (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseComplete,
  parameters: [position : Nat, stack : (List LooseFrame), value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: LooseReading,
  body: (match stack
    | List.nil => (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead position 7 (nil LooseFrame) value)
    | List.cons top below => (match top
      | LooseFrame.LooseArr acc => (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead position 5 (cons (ctor LooseFrame.LooseArr (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem value acc)) below) (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil))
      | LooseFrame.LooseObj key acc => (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead position 5 (cons (ctor LooseFrame.LooseObj 0x (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key value acc)) below) (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil)))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseClose,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, position : Nat, stack : (List LooseFrame)],
  result: LooseReading,
  body: (match stack
    | List.nil => (looseBroken position)
    | List.cons top below => (match top
      | LooseFrame.LooseArr acc => (looseComplete (position + 1) below (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr (reversedSpine acc ((prim length text : Nat) + 1))))
      | LooseFrame.LooseObj key acc => (looseComplete (position + 1) below (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj (reversedSpine acc ((prim length text : Nat) + 1)))))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseValueAt,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, position : Nat, mode : Nat, stack : (List LooseFrame), byte : Nat],
  result: LooseReading,
  body: (if (byte =? 34) then (match (looseString text (position + 1))
    | Option.some scanned => (looseComplete (first scanned) stack (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr (second scanned)))
    | Option.none => (looseBroken position)) else (if (byte =? 116) then (if (prim equal (LexLeanStd.Text::sliceOr text position 4) 0x74727565 : Bool) then (looseComplete (position + 4) stack (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool true)) else (looseBroken position)) else (if (byte =? 102) then (if (prim equal (LexLeanStd.Text::sliceOr text position 5) 0x66616c7365 : Bool) then (looseComplete (position + 5) stack (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool false)) else (looseBroken position)) else (if (byte =? 91) then (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead (position + 1) 1 (cons (ctor LooseFrame.LooseArr (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil)) stack) (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil)) else (if (byte =? 123) then (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead (position + 1) 3 (cons (ctor LooseFrame.LooseObj 0x (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil)) stack) (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil)) else (if ((byte =? 93) && (mode =? 1)) then (looseClose text position stack) else (if ((byte =? 45) || (isDigit byte)) then (match (looseInteger text position)
    | Option.some read => (looseComplete (first read) stack (second read))
    | Option.none => (looseBroken position)) else (looseBroken position)))))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseKeyAt,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, position : Nat, mode : Nat, stack : (List LooseFrame), byte : Nat],
  result: LooseReading,
  body: (if (byte =? 34) then (match (looseString text (position + 1))
    | Option.some scanned => (match stack
      | List.nil => (looseBroken position)
      | List.cons top below => (match top
        | LooseFrame.LooseArr acc => (looseBroken position)
        | LooseFrame.LooseObj pending acc => (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead (first scanned) 4 (cons (ctor LooseFrame.LooseObj (second scanned) acc) below) (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil))))
    | Option.none => (looseBroken position)) else (if ((byte =? 125) && (mode =? 3)) then (looseClose text position stack) else (looseBroken position))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseAfterAt,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, position : Nat, stack : (List LooseFrame), byte : Nat],
  result: LooseReading,
  body: (match stack
    | List.nil => (looseBroken position)
    | List.cons top below => (match top
      | LooseFrame.LooseArr acc => (if (byte =? 44) then (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead (position + 1) 0 stack (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil)) else (if (byte =? 93) then (looseClose text position stack) else (looseBroken position)))
      | LooseFrame.LooseObj key acc => (if (byte =? 44) then (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead (position + 1) 2 stack (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil)) else (if (byte =? 125) then (looseClose text position stack) else (looseBroken position))))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: isSpace,
  parameters: [byte : Nat],
  result: Bool,
  body: ((byte =? 32) || ((byte =? 10) || ((byte =? 13) || (byte =? 9)))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseAdvance,
  parameters: [
    text : Bytes,
    position : Nat,
    mode : Nat,
    stack : (List LooseFrame),
    done : LexLeanStd.Json::Json
  ],
  result: LooseReading,
  body: (let byte : Nat := (byteAt text position); (if (isSpace byte) then (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead (position + 1) mode stack done) else (if (mode =? 7) then (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead position (if (byte =? 256) then 8 else 9) stack done) else (if (byte =? 256) then (looseBroken position) else (if ((mode =? 0) || (mode =? 1)) then (looseValueAt text position mode stack byte) else (if ((mode =? 2) || (mode =? 3)) then (looseKeyAt text position mode stack byte) else (if (mode =? 4) then (if (byte =? 58) then (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead (position + 1) 0 stack done) else (looseBroken position)) else (looseAfterAt text position stack byte)))))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: looseStep,
  parameters: [text : Bytes, state : LooseReading],
  result: (Option LooseReading),
  body: (match state
    | LooseReading.LooseRead position mode stack done => (if (8 <=? mode) then (ctor Option.none [LooseReading]) else (ctor Option.some [LooseReading] (looseAdvance text position mode stack done)))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: readLoose,
  parameters: [text : Bytes],
  result: (Option LexLeanStd.Json::Json),
  body: (match (first (prim iterate_until (fun [text] (state : LooseReading) => (looseStep text state)) ((prim length text : Nat) + 3) (ctor LooseReading.LooseRead 0 0 (nil LooseFrame) (ctor LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil)) : (LooseReading * Bool)))
    | LooseReading.LooseRead position mode stack done => (if (mode =? 8) then (ctor Option.some [LexLeanStd.Json::Json] done) else (ctor Option.none [LexLeanStd.Json::Json]))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

structure{
  name: KeyCheck,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  fields: [{name: seen, type: (List Bytes)}, {name: valid, type: Bool}]
}

definition{
  name: lit,
  parameters: [literal : String],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (prim utf8_encode literal : Bytes),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: decStr,
  parameters: [value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option Bytes),
  body: (match value
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => (ctor Option.none [Bytes])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => (ctor Option.none [Bytes])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => (ctor Option.some [Bytes] text)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr items => (ctor Option.none [Bytes])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj members => (ctor Option.none [Bytes])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => (ctor Option.none [Bytes])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => (ctor Option.none [Bytes])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => (ctor Option.none [Bytes])),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: decBool,
  parameters: [value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option Bool),
  body: (match value
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => (ctor Option.some [Bool] flag)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => (ctor Option.none [Bool])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => (ctor Option.none [Bool])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr items => (ctor Option.none [Bool])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj members => (ctor Option.none [Bool])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => (ctor Option.none [Bool])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => (ctor Option.none [Bool])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => (ctor Option.none [Bool])),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: decNat,
  parameters: [value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (match value
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => (ctor Option.none [Nat])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => (if negative then (ctor Option.none [Nat]) else (if (9223372036854775807 <? magnitude) then (ctor Option.none [Nat]) else (ctor Option.some [Nat] magnitude)))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => (ctor Option.none [Nat])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr items => (ctor Option.none [Nat])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj members => (ctor Option.none [Nat])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => (ctor Option.none [Nat])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => (ctor Option.none [Nat])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => (ctor Option.none [Nat])),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: decNatFuel,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (decNat value),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: lookup,
  parameters: [members : LexLeanStd.Json::Json, wanted : Bytes],
  result: (Option LexLeanStd.Json::Json),
  body: (match members
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => (ctor Option.none [LexLeanStd.Json::Json])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => (ctor Option.none [LexLeanStd.Json::Json])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => (ctor Option.none [LexLeanStd.Json::Json])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr items => (ctor Option.none [LexLeanStd.Json::Json])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj nested => (ctor Option.none [LexLeanStd.Json::Json])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => (ctor Option.none [LexLeanStd.Json::Json])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => (ctor Option.none [LexLeanStd.Json::Json])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => (if (prim equal key wanted : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [LexLeanStd.Json::Json] inner) else (lookup rest wanted))),
  executable: true,
  recursive_argument: members
}

definition{
  name: keyList,
  parameters: [members : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (List Bytes),
  body: (match members
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => (nil Bytes)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => (nil Bytes)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => (nil Bytes)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr items => (nil Bytes)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj nested => (nil Bytes)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => (nil Bytes)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => (nil Bytes)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => (cons key (keyList rest))),
  executable: true,
  recursive_argument: members
}

definition{
  name: listed,
  parameters: [names : (List Bytes), wanted : Bytes],
  result: Bool,
  body: (prim list_fold (fun [wanted] (found : Bool) (candidate : Bytes) => (if found then true else (prim equal candidate wanted : Bool))) false names : Bool),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: countOf,
  parameters: [names : (List Bytes), wanted : Bytes],
  result: Nat,
  body: (prim list_fold (fun [wanted] (count : Nat) (candidate : Bytes) => (if (prim equal candidate wanted : Bool) then (count + 1) else count)) 0 names : Nat),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: checkKey,
  parameters: [allowed : (List Bytes), state : KeyCheck, key : Bytes],
  result: KeyCheck,
  body: (if (project state valid) then (if (listed allowed key) then (if (listed (project state seen) key) then (record KeyCheck {seen := (project state seen), valid := false}) else (record KeyCheck {seen := (cons key (project state seen)), valid := true})) else (record KeyCheck {seen := (project state seen), valid := false})) else state),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: closedKeys,
  parameters: [members : LexLeanStd.Json::Json, allowed : (List Bytes)],
  result: Bool,
  body: (project (prim list_fold (fun [allowed] (state : KeyCheck) (key : Bytes) => (checkKey allowed state key)) (record KeyCheck {seen := (nil Bytes), valid := true}) (keyList members) : KeyCheck) valid),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: kindOf,
  parameters: [members : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option Bytes),
  body: (if (1 <? (countOf (keyList members) (lit "kind"))) then (ctor Option.none [Bytes]) else (match (lookup members (lit "kind"))
    | Option.some found => (decStr found)
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [Bytes]))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: strItems,
  parameters: [items : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option (List Bytes)),
  body: (match items
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr nested => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj members => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => (ctor Option.some [(List Bytes)] (nil Bytes))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => (match (decStr element)
      | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
      | Option.some head => (match (strItems rest)
        | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
        | Option.some tail => (ctor Option.some [(List Bytes)] (cons head tail))))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])),
  executable: true,
  recursive_argument: items
}

definition{
  name: decNames,
  parameters: [value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option (List Bytes)),
  body: (match value
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr items => (strItems items)
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj members => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: allListed,
  parameters: [names : (List Bytes), allowed : (List Bytes)],
  result: Bool,
  body: (prim list_fold (fun [allowed] (valid : Bool) (named : Bytes) => (if valid then (listed allowed named) else false)) true names : Bool),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: decChecks,
  parameters: [value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option (List Bytes)),
  body: (match (decNames value)
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)])
    | Option.some names => (if (allListed names (cons (lit "input_invariant") (cons (lit "output_invariant") (cons (lit "postcondition") (cons (lit "precondition") (nil Bytes)))))) then (ctor Option.some [(List Bytes)] names) else (ctor Option.none [(List Bytes)]))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: decComparison,
  parameters: [value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option Bytes),
  body: (match (decStr value)
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [Bytes])
    | Option.some text => (if (listed (cons (lit "nat_beq") (cons (lit "nat_blt") (nil Bytes))) text) then (ctor Option.some [Bytes] text) else (ctor Option.none [Bytes]))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: decProduction,
  parameters: [value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option ((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))),
  body: (match value
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr items => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj members => (if (closedKeys members (cons (lit "effects") (cons (lit "targets") (nil Bytes)))) then (match (lookup members (lit "targets"))
      | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])
      | Option.some targets => (match (lookup members (lit "effects"))
        | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])
        | Option.some effects => (match (decNames targets)
          | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])
          | Option.some named => (match (decNames effects)
            | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])
            | Option.some effected => (ctor Option.some [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))] (named, effected)))))) else (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))]))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => (ctor Option.none [((List Bytes) * (List Bytes))])),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: larger,
  parameters: [left : Nat, right : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (if (left <? right) then right else left),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: depthOf,
  parameters: [value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match value
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => 0
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => 0
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => 0
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr items => (1 + (depthOf items))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj members => (1 + (depthOf members))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => 0
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => (larger (depthOf element) (depthOf rest))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => (larger (depthOf inner) (depthOf rest))),
  executable: true,
  recursive_argument: value
}

definition{
  name: nodesOf,
  parameters: [value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match value
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JBool flag => 1
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JInt negative magnitude => 1
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JStr text => 1
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JArr items => (1 + (nodesOf items))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JObj members => (1 + (nodesOf members))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JNil => 1
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JItem element rest => ((1 + (nodesOf element)) + (nodesOf rest))
    | LexLeanStd.Json::Json.JMember key inner rest => ((1 + (nodesOf inner)) + (nodesOf rest))),
  executable: true,
  recursive_argument: value
}

definition{

definition{

definition{
  name: ignoredValue,
  parameters: [

  mutual: Ignore,
  recursive_argument: fuel

definition{

definition{
  name: decAny,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, value : LexLeanStd.Json::Json],
  result: (Option SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration),
  body: (match (objectMembers value)
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration])

      | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration])

        | Option.some declared => (ctor Option.some [SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration] (ctor SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration.AnyOrdinary declared))
        | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration])) else (if (listed (cons (lit "artifact") (cons (lit "contract") (cons (lit "realization") (cons (lit "evidence") (cons (lit "model") (nil Bytes)))))) tag) then (match (decModelDeclaration fuel value)
        | Option.some declared => (ctor Option.some [SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration] (ctor SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration.AnyModel declared))
        | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration])) else (match (decReasoningDeclaration fuel value)
        | Option.some declared => (ctor Option.some [SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration] (ctor SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration.AnyReasoning declared))
        | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration])))))),
  executable: true

            | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [(Bytes * (List SemanticReasoning::AnyDeclaration))])

  executable: true

definition{
  name: decodeSemanticModule,
  parameters: [text : Bytes],
  result: Decoded,
  body: (match (readLoose text)
    | Option.none => (ctor Decoded.DInvalid)
    | Option.some json => (if (127 <? (depthOf json)) then (ctor Decoded.DInvalid) else (let fuel : Nat := ((nodesOf json) + 2); (match (decModuleValue fuel json)
      | Option.none => (ctor Decoded.DInvalid)
      | Option.some decoded => (if (prim equal (LexLeanStd.Json::writeJson json) text : Bool) then (match (ignoredValue fuel json (typedJson (first decoded) (second decoded)) (lit "$"))
        | Option.some found => (ctor Decoded.DOutside found)
        | Option.none => (ctor Decoded.DOk (first decoded) (second decoded))) else (ctor Decoded.DNotCanonical)))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true,
  production: {effects: [allocation, overflow, recursion], targets: [rust-std]}
}

\end{lexlean}
